<a id="pm-s1"></a>
## 1. Abstract and result contract

> We prove the Kirchberg–Rørdam character criterion: for every free ultrafilter on ℕ, a nonzero unital separable complex <i>C</i><sup>∗</sup>-algebra absorbs the Jiang–Su algebra if and only if its norm central-sequence algebra has no characters. We also prove that the infinite minimal tensor power of every nonzero unital separable complex <i>C</i><sup>∗</sup>-algebra without characters is $`\mathcal Z`$-stable and contains a unital copy of $`\mathcal Z`$.

- **C1** (theorem, [source](#stmt-thm-main)): Let $A$ be a nonzero unital separable complex $C^*$-algebra, and let $\omega$ be any free ultrafilter on $\mathbb N$. Then $$F_\omega(A)\text{ has no characters} \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad A\cong A\otimes_{\min}\mathcal Z.$$
  - Explanation: Absence of characters on the norm central-sequence algebra is equivalent to Jiang–Su absorption under the stated unital separable hypotheses.
- **C2** (construction, [source](#stmt-thm-finite)): Let $D$ be a nonzero unital complex $C^*$-algebra without characters. There is an integer $m\ge1$ and a unital $*$-homomorphism $$I(2,3)\longrightarrow D^{\otimes_{\max}m},$$ where $$I(2,3)=\{f\in C([0,1],M_2\otimes M_3): f(0)\in M_2\otimes1_3,\ f(1)\in1_2\otimes M_3\}.$$
  - Explanation: Absence of characters yields a unital dimension-drop homomorphism into a finite maximal tensor power; separability is not required.
- **C3** (theorem, [source](#stmt-cor-infinite-tensor)): Let $D$ be a nonzero unital separable complex $C^*$-algebra without characters, and write $$B=D^{\otimes_{\min}\infty} :=\varinjlim_{n\ge1} \bigl(D^{\otimes_{\min}n},\,x\longmapsto x\otimes1_D\bigr).$$ Then $B\cong B\otimes_{\min}\mathcal Z$, and $B$ contains a unital copy of $\mathcal Z$.
  - Explanation: The infinite minimal tensor power absorbs the Jiang–Su algebra and contains it unitally under the stated separability hypothesis.
- **C4** (construction, [source](#stmt-prop-rw)): Let $B$ be a unital $C^*$-algebra. Suppose that $a_1,a_2\in B$ are orthogonal positive contractions with $a_1=v^*v$, $a_2=vv^*$ for some $v\in B$, and that, for some $\varepsilon>0$, $$1_B-a_1-a_2\precsim(a_1-\varepsilon)_+.$$ Then there is a unital $*$-homomorphism $I(2,3)\to B$.
  - Explanation: Exact equivalence, orthogonality and the cutoff defect comparison yield a unital dimension-drop homomorphism without a stable rank assumption.
- **C5** (construction, [source](#stmt-prop-kr)): Let $A$ be a nonzero unital separable $C^*$-algebra.* 1. *If $D\subset F_\omega(A)$ is separable and contains the ambient unit, then for every $m\ge1$ there is a unital $*$-homomorphism $D^{\otimes_{\max}m}\to F_\omega(A)$.* 2. *$A\cong A\otimes_{\min}\mathcal Z$ if and only if $F_\omega(A)$ contains, with the ambient unit, a separable subhomogeneous $C^*$-algebra without characters.
  - Explanation: Separable same-unit subalgebras admit finite commuting-copy maps, and a same-unit subhomogeneous characterless subalgebra detects absorption.
- **C6** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-rw-exact-cut)): If $a,b\in B_+$ satisfy $a\precsim b$, then for every $\delta>0$ there is $z\in B$ such that $$z^*z=(a-\delta)_+, \qquad zz^*\in\operatorname{Her}(b).$$
  - Explanation: A positive cutoff is the initial positive product of an element whose final positive product lies in the prescribed hereditary subalgebra; the cutoff need not be a projection.
- **C7** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-rw-commuting-maps)): Let $\alpha:M_3\to B$ and $\beta:M_2\to B$ be completely positive contractive maps with commuting ranges, satisfying $\psi(x)\psi(y)=\psi(1)\psi(xy)$ for $\psi=\alpha,\beta$. If $\alpha(1_3)+\beta(1_2)=1_B$, there is a unital homomorphism $I(2,3)\to B$.
  - Explanation: Commuting contractive order-zero matrix maps whose units sum to the ambient unit yield a dimension-drop homomorphism.
- **C8** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-tensor-independence)): Let $M\subseteq B(H)$ be a von Neumann factor with $1_M=1_H$, and let $B\subseteq M'$ be a unital algebra. The multiplication map $$M\odot B\longrightarrow B(H),\qquad a\otimes b\longmapsto ab,$$ is injective. Consequently, multiplication is injective on the complex algebraic tensor product of any finite family of commuting von Neumann factors on the same Hilbert space, all with unit $1_H$.
  - Explanation: Multiplication preserves algebraic tensor independence for commuting factors; no spatial decomposition is assumed.
- **C9** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-tensor-grouped-factors)): Let $\pi:B_1\otimes_{\max}\cdots\otimes_{\max}B_q\to B(H)$ be an irreducible representation, where the $B_j$ are nonzero unital $C^*$-algebras. The weak-operator closures of the represented tensor legs are commuting von Neumann factors. The same is true after grouping the legs according to any partition into nonempty blocks.
  - Explanation: Irreducibility forces represented tensor blocks and their weak closures to have scalar centers.
- **C10** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-tensor-detecting-space)): Let $D$ be a nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra without characters. There is a finite-dimensional real subspace $V\subseteq D_{\mathrm{sa}}$ such that the image of $V$ contains a noncommuting pair in every nonzero unital representation of $D$.
  - Explanation: A finite selfadjoint detecting space retains a noncommuting pair in every nonzero unital representation.
- **C11** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-tensor-real-labels)): Let $V$ be a finite-dimensional real vector space with $m=\dim_{\mathbb R}V\ge2$. For every integer $r\ge1$, there are $n\ge1$ and tensors $x_1,\ldots,x_r\in V^{\otimes_{\mathbb R}n}$ with the following property: whenever $P_j:V\to E_j$ are real linear maps of rank at least two, the tensors $$(P_1\otimes\cdots\otimes P_n)(x_i), \qquad 1\le i\le r,$$ are linearly independent over $\mathbb R$.
  - Explanation: A finite tensor-label family remains independent under all real maps of the prescribed minimum rank.
- **C12** (construction, [source](#stmt-cor-tensor-represented-labels)): Let $D$ and $V$ be as in Lemma <a href="#lem:tensor-detecting-space" data-reference-type="ref" data-reference="lem:tensor-detecting-space">10</a>. For each $r\ge1$, there are $n\ge1$ and selfadjoint elements $x_1,\ldots,x_r\in D^{\otimes n}$ whose images are complex linearly independent under every irreducible representation of $D^{\otimes n}\otimes_{\max}B$, for every nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra $B$. Here the elements $x_i$ occupy the first $n$ tensor legs.
  - Explanation: Real selfadjoint tensor labels remain complex independent in all the indicated irreducible represented tensor products.
- **C13** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-tensor-full-square-zero)): If $D$ is a nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra without characters, then some finite maximal tensor power $P$ of $D$ contains a full element $w$ satisfying $w^2=0$ and $\\|w\\|=1$.
  - Explanation: A finite maximal tensor power contains a normalized full square-zero element.
- **C14** (construction, [source](#stmt-prop-full-cone)): For every nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra $D$ without characters, there are an integer $m\ge1$, the maximal tensor power $P=D^{\otimes m}$, and a unital $*$-homomorphism $\eta:C\to P$ such that $\eta(\iota e_{11})$ is full in $P$.
  - Explanation: The square-zero construction supplies a unitized matrix-cone homomorphism with a full first corner.
- **C15** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-cone-cube)): For $k\ge1$, the algebra $C^{\otimes k}$ identifies with $$\left\{ f\in C([0,1]^k,M_2^{\otimes k}): f(t)\in\bigotimes_{i=1}^k E_i(t) \right\}, \qquad E_i(t)= \begin{cases} M_2,&t_i>0,\\ \mathbb C1,&t_i=0. \end{cases}$$
  - Explanation: Matrix-valued cube sections describe the cone tensor power with scalar boundary restrictions at vanishing coordinates.
- **C16** (construction, [source](#stmt-lem-matrix-simplex)): For every $k\ge1$, there is a point-norm continuous family of unital $*$-homomorphisms $$\theta_r:M_2\longrightarrow M_2^{\otimes k}, \qquad r\in\Delta_{k-1} =\left\{r\in[0,1]^k:\sum_i r_i=1\right\},$$ whose range uses only the tensor factors indexed by $\{i:r_i>0\}$. At the $j$-th vertex, $\theta_r$ is the $j$-th coordinate inclusion.
  - Explanation: A continuous family moves the matrix inclusion among tensor factors while respecting simplex faces.
- **C17** (construction, [source](#stmt-prop-cone-drop)): Let $P$ be a nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra. Suppose that there is a unital $*$-homomorphism $\eta:C\to P$ such that $\eta(\iota e_{11})$ is full in $P$. Then, for some finite $m\ge1$, the maximal tensor power $B=P^{\otimes m}$ contains orthogonal positive contractions $b_1,b_2$ and an element $v_B$ such that $$b_1=v_B^*v_B,\qquad b_2=v_Bv_B^*,\qquad 1_B-b_1-b_2\precsim(b_1-1/2)_+.$$ Consequently there is a unital $*$-homomorphism $$I(2,3)\longrightarrow P^{\otimes m}.$$
  - Explanation: A full cone corner yields an exactly equivalent orthogonal pair satisfying the fixed-cutoff defect comparison after finite maximal amplification.
- **C18** (theorem, [source](#stmt-lem-separable)): Every nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra $B$ without characters contains a separable $C^*$-subalgebra $D$ without characters and with $1_D=1_B$.
  - Explanation: A finite commutator witness produces a separable characterless subalgebra sharing the ambient unit.

```json
          "source_id": "canonical:PM-299.1"
        }
      ],
      "statement_anchor": "stmt-cor-infinite-tensor"
    },

      "conclusion": {
        "anchor": "stmt-prop-kr",
        "exact_quote": "Let $A$ be a nonzero unital separable $C^*$-algebra.*\n\n1.  *If $D\\subset F_\\omega(A)$ is separable and contains the ambient unit, then for every $m\\ge1$ there is a unital $*$-homomorphism $D^{\\otimes_{\\max}m}\\to F_\\omega(A)$.*\n\n2.  *$A\\cong A\\otimes_{\\min}\\mathcal Z$ if and only if $F_\\omega(A)$ contains, with the ambient unit, a separable subhomogeneous $C^*$-algebra without characters.",
        "source_id": "canonical:PM-299.1"
      },
      "evidence": [
```

<a id="pm-s2"></a>
## 2. Definitions, validity and bounds

- **Ultrapower and norm central-sequence algebra** ([source](#sec:introduction)): For a nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra $A$ and a free ultrafilter $\omega$ on $\mathbb N$, write $$A_\omega=\ell^\infty(\mathbb N,A)\big/ \{(a_n):\lim_{n\to\omega}\\|a_n\\|=0\}, \qquad F_\omega(A)=A_\omega\cap A',$$ where $A$ is identified with its diagonal image in $A_\omega$.
- **Character** ([source](#sec:introduction)): A *character* is a nonzero $*$-homomorphism to $\mathbb C$; on a unital algebra it is automatically unital.
- **Jiang–Su stability** ([source](#sec:introduction)): An algebra is *$\mathcal Z$-stable* if it is isomorphic to its minimal tensor product with $\mathcal Z$.
- **Subhomogeneity** ([source](#sec:introduction)): Here subhomogeneity means a common finite bound on the dimensions of irreducible representations.
- **Full element** ([source](#pm-h-6)): An element is *full* if its generated closed two-sided ideal is the whole algebra.
- **Cuntz subequivalence** ([source](#pm-h-6)): For positive elements in finite matrix algebras over a unital algebra $B$, write $a\precsim b$ if, after adjoining zero corners as necessary, there are matrices $x_j$ with $x_j^*b x_j\to a$ in norm.
- **Cuntz class** ([source](#pm-h-6)): Write $[a]$ for the resulting Cuntz equivalence class.
- **Exact equivalence of positive elements** ([source](#pm-h-7)): We say that positive elements $a_1,a_2\in B$ are *equivalent* if $a_1=v^*v$ and $a_2=vv^*$ for one $v\in B$.
- **Hereditary subalgebra** ([source](#sec:rw)): For $b\ge0$, write $\operatorname{Her}(b)=\overline{bBb}$.
- **Unitized matrix cone** ([source](#pm-h-14)): Let $$C=\{f\in C([0,1],M_2):f(0)\in\mathbb C1_{M_2}\}, \qquad \iota(t)=t.$$
- **Dimension-drop algebra** ([source](#stmt-thm-finite)): Let $D$ be a nonzero unital complex $C^*$-algebra without characters. There is an integer $m\ge1$ and a unital $*$-homomorphism $$I(2,3)\longrightarrow D^{\otimes_{\max}m},$$ where $$I(2,3)=\{f\in C([0,1],M_2\otimes M_3): f(0)\in M_2\otimes1_3,\ f(1)\in1_2\otimes M_3\}.$$
- **Infinite minimal tensor power** ([source](#stmt-cor-infinite-tensor)): Let $D$ be a nonzero unital separable complex $C^*$-algebra without characters, and write $$B=D^{\otimes_{\min}\infty} :=\varinjlim_{n\ge1} \bigl(D^{\otimes_{\min}n},\,x\longmapsto x\otimes1_D\bigr).$$ Then $B\cong B\otimes_{\min}\mathcal Z$, and $B$ contains a unital copy of $\mathcal Z$.

| Q1 | Finite tensor exponent minimum | ≥ | $1$ | The exponent depends on the algebra. |

| Q4 | Normalized square-zero norm | = | $1$ | Normalization of a nonzero element. |
| Q5 | Fullness approximation tolerance | < | $\frac12$ | Finite fullness witnesses. |
| Q6 | Defect cutoff | = | $1/2$ | Finite maximal amplification. |
| Q7 | Flip-path lower transition | = | $1/3$ | Interval transition in the dimension-drop proof. |
| Q8 | Flip-path upper transition | = | $2/3$ | Interval transition in the dimension-drop proof. |
| Q9 | Auxiliary tensor amplification condition | ≥ | $N$ | Comparison multiplicity from fullness. |
| Q10 | Cube coordinate count | = | $N^L$ | Comparison multiplicity and chosen auxiliary exponent. |
| Q11 | Separable-reduction approximation tolerance | < | $1$ | Finite commutator witnesses. |
| Q12 | Irreducible fiber dimension at the origin | = | $2$ | Evaluation at the origin. |
| Q13 | Irreducible fiber dimension at the other endpoint | = | $3$ | Evaluation at the other endpoint. |
| Q14 | Irreducible fiber dimension at the interior | = | $6$ | Evaluation at the interior. |

```json
          "source_id": "canonical:PM-299.1"
        }
```

<a id="pm-s3"></a>
## 3. Proof roadmap

- **R1** [claim](#stmt-lem-tensor-detecting-space) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-tensor-detecting-space): Finite commutator witnesses for the unit ideal. -> Finite real selfadjoint detecting space.
- **R2** [claim](#stmt-lem-tensor-real-labels) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-tensor-real-labels): Incidence manifold dimension and Sard’s theorem. -> Representation-stable real tensor labels.
- **R3** [claim](#stmt-cor-tensor-represented-labels) / [proof](#proof-stmt-cor-tensor-represented-labels): Complexification and multiplication independence of commuting factors. -> Complex independent selfadjoint labels.
- **R4** [claim](#stmt-lem-tensor-full-square-zero) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-tensor-full-square-zero): Double commutator sign obstruction and independent bridge labels. -> Full normalized square-zero element.
- **R5** [claim](#stmt-prop-full-cone) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-full-cone): Polar decomposition and continuous functional calculus. -> Unital matrix-cone map with full first corner.
- **R6** [claim](#stmt-prop-cone-drop) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-cone-drop): Cube support localization and auxiliary Cuntz amplification. -> Exactly equivalent orthogonal pair with cutoff defect comparison.
- **R7** [claim](#stmt-thm-finite) / [proof](#proof-stmt-prop-cone-drop-2): Composition of the cone construction and dimension-drop criterion. -> Unital dimension-drop map into a finite maximal tensor power.
- **R8** [claim](#stmt-thm-main) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-separable-2): Separable reduction, commuting copies and the subhomogeneous-image absorption criterion. -> Jiang–Su absorption and the character criterion.
- **R9** [claim](#stmt-cor-infinite-tensor) / [proof](#proof-stmt-lem-separable-3): Maximal-to-minimal quotient, finite-stage inclusion and infinite tensor absorption. -> Stable infinite minimal tensor power with a unital Jiang–Su copy.

<!-- PM:BEGIN pm.roadmap.v2 -->

<a id="pm-s4"></a>
## 4. Engineering Triptych

### Panel 1 - epistemic contract

- **I_character_criterion**: Absence of characters on the norm central-sequence algebra is equivalent to Jiang–Su absorption.
- **I_finite_map**: A finite maximal tensor power admits a unital dimension-drop homomorphism.
- **I_infinite_absorption**: The infinite minimal tensor power absorbs and contains a unital copy of the Jiang–Su algebra.
- **I_cube_pair**: The cube construction gives an exactly equivalent orthogonal positive pair with scalar sum.
- **I_local_support**: The first pair element acts identically on the localized coordinate cuts.

### Panel 2 - negative knowledge

- **A_finite_absorption**: Conclude Jiang–Su absorption for the finite tensor power. -> The finite conclusion supplies a homomorphism rather than finite-power absorption.
- **A_tensor_norm**: Interchange maximal and minimal tensor products throughout the construction. -> Nonspatial represented tensor factors need not admit a spatial decomposition.
- **A_hypotheses**: Apply the character criterion without separability or unitality. -> The theorem does not license the conclusion outside its stated algebra regime.
- **A_finite_test**: Treat an exact cube example as a test deciding the full absorption theorem. -> Matrix identities do not decide all representations or the norm central-sequence algebra.

### Panel 3 - reference implementation and oracle

Applicability: `constructive`. The cube proof gives explicit matrix-valued functions. This implementation constructs its orthogonal pair and localized cut on the single-coordinate face with an exact rational square-root regime; it does not decide absorption, arbitrary tensor labels, or Cuntz comparison in a general algebra.

```python
# INPUTS: JSON object with rational strings delta and t.
# PRECONDITIONS: 0 < delta < 1; 0 <= t <= 1; H=min(2*t/delta,1) is a rational square; k=1.
# OUTPUTS: Exact rational matrices a1, a2, v, X, y as row-major lists.
# TIME: A fixed number of rational operations and integer square roots; bit cost depends on input bit length.
# SPACE: A fixed number of integers whose bit lengths depend on the input.
# ARITHMETIC: Exact python-flint fmpq arithmetic and integer isqrt; no floating point.
# FAILURE: Invalid regime or non-square H raises ValueError; no approximate witness is returned.
import json, sys
from math import isqrt
from flint import fmpq
p = json.loads(sys.stdin.read())
delta, t = fmpq(p['delta']), fmpq(p['t'])
if not (0 < delta < 1 and 0 <= t <= 1):
    raise ValueError('outside regime')
H = min(fmpq(2)*t/delta, fmpq(1))
num, den = int(H.p), int(H.q)
rn, rd = isqrt(num), isqrt(den)
if rn*rn != num or rd*rd != den:
    raise ValueError('cutoff is not a rational square')
s = fmpq(rn, rd)
g = fmpq(1)-H
q = max(t-delta, fmpq(0))
z = fmpq(0)
a1 = [H,z,z,z]
a2 = [z,z,z,H]
v = [z,z,s,z]
X = [g,z,z,g]
y = [q,z,z,z]
print(json.dumps({name:[str(x) for x in vals] for name,vals in [('a1',a1),('a2',a2),('v',v),('X',X),('y',y)]}, sort_keys=True))
```

<a id="pm-s6"></a>
## 6. Applicability limits and search

- **L1** (hypothesis): The absorption equivalence requires a nonzero unital separable complex algebra and a free ultrafilter; it concerns the norm central-sequence algebra. Blocked use: ['Applying the equivalence after dropping its algebra or ultrafilter hypotheses.']
- **L2** (formal_scope): Norm central sequences cannot be replaced by a tracial ultrapower in the stated criterion. Blocked use: ['Treating a tracial ultrapower as the central-sequence algebra of the theorem.']
- **L3** (hypothesis): The finite construction requires a nonzero unital complex algebra without characters; separability is unnecessary for that construction. Blocked use: ['Applying the finite theorem to a zero algebra, a nonunital algebra, or an algebra with characters.']
- **L4** (counterexample_scope): A unital dimension-drop homomorphism into a finite tensor power is not a conclusion of Jiang–Su absorption for that finite power. Blocked use: ['Inferring finite-power absorption from the finite theorem.']
- **L5** (edge_case): The homomorphism may have a kernel; its subhomogeneous characterless image is the property used in the absorption application. Blocked use: ['Claiming that the finite dimension-drop map is injective.']
- **L6** (hypothesis): The infinite minimal tensor-power absorption statement requires separability as well as nonzeroness, unitality, complex scalars and absence of characters. Blocked use: ['Extending the corollary to arbitrary nonseparable inputs without further justification.']
- **L7** (formal_scope): The finite construction uses maximal tensor products; the infinite absorption consequence uses minimal tensor products. Blocked use: ['Silently interchanging tensor norms in the construction.']
- **L8** (unknown): The tensor exponents depend on the detecting space and bridge family; a bound uniform over the algebra is not asserted. Blocked use: ['Publishing a uniform tensor exponent or an algebra-independent cost bound.']
- **L9** (nonconstructive): The representation-stable labels are chosen outside a measure-zero projection; this argument supplies existence without an effective general selection procedure. Blocked use: ['Presenting the label existence proof as an executed algorithm for arbitrary input algebras.']
- **L10** (formal_scope): The dimension-drop criterion requires exact equivalence through a common element and the cutoff defect comparison, beyond orthogonality or fullness alone. Blocked use: ['Replacing exact equivalence by equality of Cuntz classes alone.', 'Inferring the dimension-drop conclusion solely from a full first cone corner without the further comparison construction.']
- **L11** (edge_case): Support projections and polar decompositions may lie in the bidual; membership in the original algebra requires the supplied arguments. Blocked use: ['Treating bidual support projections or polar factors as elements of the original algebra without justification.']
- **L12** (formal_scope): The dimension-drop implication used here does not assume stable rank one. Blocked use: ['Adding stable rank one as a necessary hypothesis of this implication.']
- **L13** (edge_case): The defect construction also covers a zero equivalence witness and does not require a nonzero defect. Blocked use: ['Excluding the zero-defect boundary case from the dimension-drop criterion.']
- **L14** (formal_scope): The independence argument does not assume faithful tensor-leg restrictions or a spatial decomposition of the represented factors. Blocked use: ['Restricting the tensor representation argument to spatial representations.']
- **L15** (approximation): Cuntz comparison is an exact norm-limit assertion; finite numerical samples do not establish it for arbitrary positive elements. Blocked use: ['Using floating-point sampling as a certificate of the general defect comparison.']

```json
          "quote": "Let $P$ be a nonzero unital $C^*$-algebra. Suppose that there is a unital $*$-homomorphism $\\eta:C\\to P$ such that $\\eta(\\iota e_{11})$ is full in $P$. Then, for some finite $m\\ge1$, the maximal tensor power $B=P^{\\otimes m}$ contains orthogonal positive contractions $b_1,b_2$ and an element $v_B$ such that $$b_1=v_B^*v_B,\\qquad b_2=v_Bv_B^*,\\qquad\n1_B-b_1-b_2\\precsim(b_1-1/2)_+.$$ Consequently there is a unital $*$-homomorphism $$I(2,3)\\longrightarrow P^{\\otimes m}.$$",
          "quote_sha256": "adaf4b05eb02d59afd9c0d8757bf9c6cb41d35729d4699dbaee2ca8e86953200"
        }
```

<a id="pm-formal"></a>
### Formal statement (Lean 4)

Corpus status: formalized in the source corpus (toolchain `leanprover/lean4:v4.34.1`, Mathlib `d13f23b723b8a846827a245b89c10fc7d3f11612`). Not re-checked here; the statements give the exact quantifiers and hypotheses, not a proof.

Scope note of family 299 (`lean/docs/299.md`, verbatim):

> The following describes the scope of the Lean formalization related to the following accompanying paper(s):
>
> - [The Kirchberg–Rørdam character criterion](../../preprints/The-Kirchberg-Rordam-character-criterion-September-25-2026/paper.pdf)
>
> ## Scope
>
> The Kirchberg–Rørdam criterion relates Jiang–Su absorption to characters of the central-sequence algebra. The formalized result proves that, for every nonzero unital separable complex $C^*$-algebra $A$ and every free ultrafilter on $\mathbb N$, the norm central-sequence algebra has no nonzero character exactly when $A\cong A\otimes_{\min}\mathcal Z$. No nuclearity, simplicity, trace, or comparison hypothesis is imposed.

```lean
structure System where
  obj : ℕ → Type u
  [cstar : ∀ n, CStarAlgebra (obj n)]
  map : ∀ i j, i ≤ j → obj i →⋆ₐ[ℂ] obj j
  map_self : ∀ i x, map i i le_rfl x = x
  map_map : ∀ i j k (hij : i ≤ j) (hjk : j ≤ k) x,
    map j k hjk (map i j hij x) = map i k (hij.trans hjk) x
  norm_map : ∀ i j (h : i ≤ j) x, ‖map i j h x‖ = ‖x‖
```

```lean
structure CrossSeminorm extends Seminorm ℂ (A ⊗[ℂ] B) where
  mul_le : ∀ x y, toSeminorm (x * y) ≤ toSeminorm x * toSeminorm y
  star_eq : ∀ x, toSeminorm (star x) = toSeminorm x
  cstar_eq : ∀ x, toSeminorm (star x * x) = toSeminorm x * toSeminorm x
  tmul_le : ∀ a b, toSeminorm (a ⊗ₜ[ℂ] b) ≤ ‖a‖ * ‖b‖
```

```lean
structure Data (p q P Q : ℕ) where
  lower : ℕ
  upper : ℕ
  middle : ℕ
  zeroEnd : ℕ
  zeroMid : ℕ
  oneEnd : ℕ
  oneMid : ℕ
  lower_pos : 0<lower
  upper_pos : 0<upper
  zero_low : q*lower=zeroEnd*Q
  zero_mid : upper+middle=zeroMid*Q
  one_low : p*upper=P*oneEnd
  one_mid : lower+middle=P*oneMid
  zero_dim : p*zeroEnd+p*q*zeroMid=P
  one_dim : q*oneEnd+p*q*oneMid=Q
```

```lean
structure PrimeDropPresentation where
  size : ℕ → ℕ
  exponent : ℕ → ℕ
  size_power : ∀ n, size n=2^(exponent n+1)
  stepData : ∀ n, GeneralDropSystem.Data (size n) (size n+1) (size (n+1)) (size (n+1)+1)
  zero_copies : ∀ n, 2≤(stepData n).zeroEnd+(size n+1)*(stepData n).zeroMid
  one_copies : ∀ n, 2≤(stepData n).oneEnd+size n*(stepData n).oneMid
```

```json
      "statement_lean": "structure CrossSeminorm extends Seminorm ℂ (A ⊗[ℂ] B) where\n  mul_le : ∀ x y, toSeminorm (x * y) ≤ toSeminorm x * toSeminorm y\n  star_eq : ∀ x, toSeminorm (star x) = toSeminorm x\n  cstar_eq : ∀ x, toSeminorm (star x * x) = toSeminorm x * toSeminorm x\n  tmul_le : ∀ a b, toSeminorm (a ⊗ₜ[ℂ] b) ≤ ‖a‖ * ‖b‖"
```

<a id="pm-s7"></a>
## 7. Complete mathematical paper


We prove the Kirchberg–Rørdam character criterion: for every free ultrafilter on ℕ, a nonzero unital separable complex <i>C</i><sup>∗</sup>-algebra absorbs the Jiang–Su algebra if and only if its norm central-sequence algebra has no characters. We also prove that the infinite minimal tensor power of every nonzero unital separable complex <i>C</i><sup>∗</sup>-algebra without characters is $`\mathcal Z`$-stable and contains a unital copy of $`\mathcal Z`$.
